German social insurance funds are facing financial distress, prompting policymakers to consider a new &\#x27;Pflegesoli&\#x27;—a surcharge on privately insured individuals—that would channel revenue to the public nursing-care system. The proposal, framed as a &\#x27;Finanzausgleich&\#x27; \(financial equalization\) of private nursing-care insurance, would require well-earning private insurers to contribute toward the social care funds. The idea is being discussed not only among social-policy advocates but also within the federal government. The article, reported by Dietrich Creutzburg in Berlin for the Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung, identifies the scheme as a mechanism to ease pressure on the public system by tapping the private-insured cohort. No specific contribution rates, legislative timelines, or implementation details have yet been confirmed.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 10, 05:29

**「German dual care insurance system」** Germany&\#x27;s long-term care system operates as a dual structure: most residents are covered by the statutory social care insurance \(gesetzliche Pflegeversicherung\), funded through mandatory payroll contributions, while high earners and civil servants may instead purchase private long-term care insurance \(private Pflegeversicherung\), which has historically been exempt from contributing to the public social insurance funds. The proposed &\#x27;Pflegesoli&\#x27;—a solidarity contribution or financial equalization mechanism—would require private insurers to make payments into the public system to offset growing deficits in the statutory care insurance driven by demographic aging and rising per-capita care costs.

**「Impact on privately insured individuals」** Privately insured Germans, particularly well-earning individuals, may soon be required to pay a new solidarity contribution \(&quot;Pflegesoli&quot;\) to subsidize the publicly funded social care insurance system, which is currently in financial distress. The measure remains at the proposal and government-consideration stage, with no confirmed legislation or timeline yet.

### [Italy Releases 130,000 Octopuses to Control Invasive Blue Crab Population](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/wissen/erde-klima/blaukrabbenplage-in-der-adria-oktopusse-als-natuerliche-feinde-201194344.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Italian researchers have released 130,000 octopuses into the Adriatic Sea as a targeted biological intervention to combat a growing plague of invasive blue crabs. Marine biologist Laura Gentile, who leads the effort, describes the project as an attempt to restore a sensitive ecological balance that had been disrupted by the non-native crustaceans. The octopuses serve as natural predators of the blue crabs, providing a means of population control without chemical or mechanical removal. The approach relies on reintroducing a missing predator link into the food web rather than relying on direct harvest or toxin-based management. The initiative was reported by the Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung, with Gentile explaining the ecological reasoning behind using octopuses as a scalable, self-sustaining countermeasure.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 10, 13:19

**「The Invasive Blue Crab」** The blue crab \(Callinectes sapidus\), a native of the North American Atlantic coast, has spread as an invasive species into the Mediterranean and has even been detected in the North and Baltic Seas. In its introduced range it faces few natural predators and can outcompete native invertebrate populations, making it a serious threat to local marine ecosystems. The project led by biologist Laura Gentile represents an attempt to use octopuses—as natural predators of crabs—as a form of targeted biological control to restore ecological balance.

**「Impact on Adriatic Fisheries」** The invasive blue crab has already devastated local fisheries in the Adriatic Sea, making the release of 130,000 octopuses a direct ecological intervention aimed at protecting Italian and regional fishing communities from further economic and environmental loss. The long-term effectiveness of this biocontrol approach remains unconfirmed.

Alexandra Pozharskaya, deputy head of the People&\#x27;s Front &quot;In Defense of Borrowers&quot; project and the &quot;Mosholovka&quot; anti-fraud platform, reported that scammers are compromising the accounts of employees at educational institutions to demand money from parents in group chats under the guise of extra expenses. To make the requests convincing, fraudsters use artificial intelligence to synthesize the voices of school administrators and teachers. The attack vector targets the social trust in established parent-teacher communication channels, making it harder for recipients to distinguish legitimate requests from scams. Pozharskaya&\#x27;s warning, published via TASS, highlights a growing trend in which AI-generated audio deepfakes are weaponized for financial extortion against school communities.

telegram · tass\_agency · Sep 10, 23:09

**「Voice phishing and AI voice cloning」** Voice phishing \(vishing\) is a social-engineering fraud technique in which attackers impersonate trusted individuals via synthetic or recorded voice to solicit money or personal data. Modern AI voice-cloning tools can produce a convincing imitation of a person&\#x27;s voice from as little as a few dozen seconds of recorded audio, which dramatically lowers the skill barrier for this type of impersonation. In Russia, the Civic Front consumer-protection platform &quot;Moshelovka&quot; \(&quot;fraud trap&quot;\) monitors and publicizes emerging scam schemes, including this new variant targeting school parent chat groups.

**「Impact」** Parents receiving money demands in school group chats should now independently verify requests through official school channels before transferring funds, as AI-cloned voices make audio confirmation unreliable. This raises the burden of verification on families who previously trusted voice-confirmed messages from known educators.

<a id="item-tech-news-455"></a>
### [Denisovan Fossils Identified in Yunnan Cave via Ancient Protein Analysis](https://telegram.me/science/4357) ⭐️ ?/10

Researchers analyzed over 60,000 bone fragments from Bianfu Cave on China&\#x27;s Yunnan–Guizhou Plateau and, using ancient protein analysis on pieces too fragmented to identify by shape, molecularly identified three bone fragments and two teeth as Denisovan, dating to approximately 167,000–134,000 years ago. The assemblage includes a partial radius, a postcranial bone that had been almost entirely absent from the confirmed Denisovan record, and is therefore critical for reconstructing body form below the skull. The cave is now the richest confirmed Denisovan site outside the original Denisova Cave in Siberia and sits in southwestern China, a natural corridor linking East Asia, the Tibetan Plateau, South Asia, and Southeast Asia—precisely the region expected to have served as a Denisovan migration and interbreeding pathway. The discovery narrows a long-standing geographic gap between the sparse Siberian fossils and the substantial Denisovan DNA carried by modern populations in Southeast Asia and Oceania.

telegram · science · Sep 10, 08:52

**「What Are Denisovans?」** Denisovans are an extinct archaic human lineage first identified in 2010 from a tiny finger bone and teeth recovered from Denisova Cave in the Altai Mountains of Siberia, with their species status established primarily through ancient DNA rather than skull morphology. Modern populations in Southeast Asia and Oceania carry measurable Denisovan genetic ancestry, yet confirmed Denisovan fossils had been exceedingly rare before this discovery, leaving the group&\#x27;s postcranial anatomy—body structure below the skull—almost entirely unknown. Proteomic \(ancient-protein\) analysis of fragmented bones has now become a key method for identifying Denisovan remains that are too damaged for genetic or morphological assessment.

**「Impact on Denisovan Research」** The molecular identification of a partial radius and four other Denisovan fragments at Bianfu Cave provides the first securely identified postcranial Denisovan remains, enabling paleoanthropologists to begin reconstructing Denisovan body structure below the skull for the first time and directly constraining models of Denisovan dispersal through the East Asia–Tibetan Plateau–Southeast Asia corridor where modern populations carry Denisovan DNA. Although the five identifiable specimens come from over 60,000 analyzed fragments, the site now stands as the richest confirmed Denisovan assemblage outside Siberia&\#x27;s Denisova Cave \(tool-2-1, tool-2-2, tool-2-3\).

<a id="item-ai-creator-3"></a>
### [Research Shows GPT-6-Astra&\#x27;s Reasoning Improves Dramatically When Prompts Are Padded with Filler Tokens](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/uvhuZHFtrgk8kNiZc/astra-is-much-better-at-reasoning-with-filler-tokens-than) ⭐️ ?/10

Dylan Xu \(posted on LessWrong, building on Redwood Research&\#x27;s filler-token eval methodology\) measured GPT-6-Astra&\#x27;s accuracy on tasks where the prompt is padded with a variable number of meaningless tokens and the model is instructed to answer immediately without reasoning. On a 4-hop natural-facts retrieval benchmark, Astra&\#x27;s score rose from roughly 10% to ~50% as filler tokens increased up to 4,096; on older AIME problems it improved from ~60% to ~90%, with benefits peaking around 8,192 filler tokens. Comparable models tested alongside it—Opus 4.5, Opus 5, GPT-5.6-Sol, and DeepSeek-V3.2—did not show a similar magnitude of improvement on the same filler-token scaling. The author frames the finding as a monitoring concern: Astra appears to perform substantial internal cognition that it does not verbalize in its chain-of-thought, making it harder to audit via CoT inspection alone. Code and results are published in a Redwood Research GitHub repository.

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 10, 22:21

**「Why it matters now」** The result directly tests an assumption underlying current lab safety cases: that a no-CoT \(no chain-of-thought\) evaluation captures the full scope of a model&\#x27;s cognitive work. If a model can complete significant serial reasoning silently—triggered by trivial filler tokens—then monitors that rely on inspecting the verbalized chain may miss capabilities or misaligned intermediate steps. The author explicitly recommends that future no-reasoning evaluations be re-run with filler tokens to elicit the model&\#x27;s maximum no-CoT performance. No independent replication or lab confirmation of the Astra-specific numbers has been reported in the supplied material.

**「Possible content angle」** 可做角度：用「无推理指令 vs. 内部仍在工作」的张力，解释为什么在 prompt 里塞入 4,096 个无意义 token 就能把 4-hop 事实推理从 ~10% 拉到 ~50%，以及这对当前 CoT 审计范式意味着什么——但克制地指出这是单一团队、单一模型（GPT-6-Astra）的结果，其他模型未复现同等幅度提升，结论的外推范围尚待验证。

## Technology Blog

<a id="item-tech-blog-1"></a>
### [Tom Reed on Why an Intelligence Explosion Can&\#x27;t Be Confined to a Data Centre](https://80000hours.org/podcast/episodes/the-goodhart-singularity/) ⭐️ ?/10

rss · 80000 Hours AI风险与职业 · Sep 10, 15:00

**「Background」** In a 80,000 Hours podcast episode framed around the idea of a &quot;Goodhart Singularity,&quot; Tom Reed takes up the question of whether a runaway intelligence explosion could be contained within the walls of a single data centre.

**「Solution」** Reed&\#x27;s argument, as the title states, is that an intelligence explosion is not a self-contained hardware event. The episode&\#x27;s &quot;Goodhart&quot; framing connects the claim to Goodhart&\#x27;s Law—the principle that a measure ceases to function well once it becomes the target of optimisation. By this reasoning, the optimisation loops, institutional incentives, and social systems that drive and absorb AI capability extend far beyond any facility&\#x27;s physical boundary, so the explosion, if it occurs, is irreducibly coupled to the wider world rather than wall-off-able inside a data centre.

**「Takeaway」** Reed&\#x27;s core thesis is that a superintelligence transition, should it happen, will be a systemic and social event that no single facility can contain, making the data-centre containment metaphor fundamentally inapt.
